# Agents · H · part 3

969 entries, heap-analyst to hive-write-balanced. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **heap-analyst**: Works a heap dump end to end and returns only the findings — what is holding the memory, with class names, retained bytes and the GC-root paths that make each claim checkable. Delegate a whole heap question to it rather than reading histograms and dominator trees in the main conversation. It reports figures; it never maps them to source, edits anything, or decides what to change. ([petrbouda/jeffrey](https://github.com/petrbouda/jeffrey/blob/HEAD/build/plugin/agents/heap-analyst.md) · ★81 · 3 copies)
- **heap-analyst**: Specialist for heap dump analysis — retained sizes, dominator tree, GC root paths, known leak detectors, graph-based clusters, collection waste, duplicate subgraphs, heap-to-heap diffs, and correlating retained objects with JFR allocation sites. Dispatch for any .hprof file, OutOfMemoryError, or memory that never comes back after GC. ([btraceio/jafar-perf-box](https://github.com/btraceio/jafar-perf-box/blob/HEAD/plugins/jafar-perf/agents/heap-analyst.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)

([mrveiss/AutoBot-AI](https://github.com/mrveiss/AutoBot-AI/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/helper-agents-guide.md) · ★11)
- **helper-curator**: Validate duplicate-helper candidates with Graphify and Serena and move proven shared behavior to the correct common module. ([CountJung/KISAutoTrade](https://github.com/CountJung/KISAutoTrade/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/helper-curator.agent.md))
- **helper-draft**: Delegate drafting jobs to this agent in tests. ([joaoribasio/alterbrain](https://github.com/joaoribasio/alterbrain/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/scripts/validate/good/claude/agents/helper-draft.md) · ★3)
- **helper-draft**: Use to produce notes, summaries, explanations, research write-ups and edits that the caller specifies, writing only to the paths the caller names. Not for text in the user's own voice that leaves the computer (use ghostwriter) and not for critique (use helper-review). ([joaoribasio/alterbrain](https://github.com/joaoribasio/alterbrain/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/helper-draft.md) · ★3)
- **helper-fable-low**: Fable 5.2 at low effort: Cory's 11-12 trial on real work, to see if Fable's own intelligence needs less thinking (cheaper, faster). Judged on cause found, fix green first try, time and cost against Opus at high; a miss is rerun at medium. ([IssisX/ScraperX](https://github.com/IssisX/ScraperX/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/helper-fable-low.md) · ★1)
- **helper-high**: The integrator's Opus helper at high effort for an ordinary non-physics job (Cory 11-13: picked per job; plain `helper` runs at the session's effort): UI, tools, the switchboard, CI, sound, notes, long reads (Cory 11-06 11:30 AM). Physics, forces, her limits, world geometry and protected test changes go to helper-physics instead. A job it fails (a red proof it cannot fix, or a real review finding) goes again on helper-max. ([IssisX/ScraperX](https://github.com/IssisX/ScraperX/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/helper-high.md) · ★1)
- **helper-high**: ModelPilot helper mode at high effort. Use only when a ModelPilot helper-mode note names it. ([louiskoide/ModelPilot](https://github.com/louiskoide/ModelPilot/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/helper-high.md))
- **helper-judgement**: Use only for named judgement passes: thesis options, the devil's advocate lens, consolidating a critique round, voice-profile calibration, self-build safety review of a medium or high risk proposal. Writes at most the one file the caller names. If it cannot run (plan limits), the main session does the pass and says so. ([joaoribasio/alterbrain](https://github.com/joaoribasio/alterbrain/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/helper-judgement.md) · ★3)
- **helper-judgement**: Delegate one judgement pass to this agent in tests. ([joaoribasio/alterbrain](https://github.com/joaoribasio/alterbrain/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/scripts/validate/good/claude/agents/helper-judgement.md) · ★3)
- **helper-lite**: Haiku, the lighter tier of `helper`: mechanical work with no judgment, fully spelled out by the brief — counts, fields and lines out of a transcript, log or json; 5+ screenshots to text; a rename, string swap or value table across named files. Returns the answer or the files touched, plus the command that proves it. Debugging, verifying, waits or anything the brief leaves open go to `helper` or the caller. - **herdr-version-upgrade**: Audit every stable Herdr release (`v*` tags in [`herdrdev/herdr`](https://github.com/herdrdev/herdr/releases)), including patches. Skip preview builds.
